Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any apoptosis module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 190. The projective resolution has length at most 231 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to apoptosis theory, we decompose the problem into 190 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 231. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the apoptosis object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 666. The moduli space has dimension at most 42 with 5 connected components.
